- `Commands::Statistics` 注册：`command_test::statistics_test` `statistics_command_registered`。
- help 含 `STATISTICS_EXAMPLES`：`compat_help_examples_banner` 断言示例段存在（见 `Verification`）。
- `--server` 默认 URL：统计测试断言默认 Mega2 base URL（测试 `statistics_server_default`）。
- `--repo` required：缺少值时 parser 拒绝（测试 `statistics_repo_required`）。
- `--repo` no-default contract：缺少配置时不得推导本地 repo target（测试 `statistics_repo_has_no_default`）。
- `--repo` path segment：有效值限制为 ADR-CS-09 单一路径段（测试 `statistics_repo_single_path_segment`）。
- remote repo source：CLI 不从 `repo_root` 或本地 repo id 补出目标（测试 `statistics_repo_not_derived_from_local_repo`）。
- `--namespace` 默认值：统计测试断言默认 namespace（测试 `statistics_namespace_default`）。
- `--dry-run` flag：help/parser 接受 dry-run preview 模式（测试 `statistics_dry_run_flag`）。
- token priority：统计测试断言 `--token-file` > `LIBRA_MEGA2_TOKEN` > `--token`（测试 `statistics_token_priority`）。
- preflight scope：使用 repo-scope `repo_hash_kind_without_schema_guard`（测试 `statistics_preflight_repo_scope`）。
- no schema upgrade：preflight 设 `upgrade_schema=false`（测试 `statistics_preflight_no_schema_upgrade`）。
- no pending migration: preflight 不运行 pending migrations（测试 `statistics_preflight_no_pending_migration`）。
- dry-run preflight connection uses read-only mode（测试 `statistics_preflight_readonly_connection`）。
- dry-run preflight write attempt is rejected by SQLite（测试 `statistics_preflight_write_rejected`）。
- `--dry-run` → `ReadOnly`：经 `operation_class_for_command` 断言（测试 `statistics_dry_run_readonly`）。
- `--dry-run` pretty-print envelope：调用 `push_statistics(dry_run=true)` 并以 pretty JSON 序列化完整信封（测试 `statistics_dry_run_prints_envelope`）。
- `--dry-run` 零 HTTP：mock 计数器为 0（测试 `statistics_dry_run_zero_http`）。
- `--dry-run` zero operation writes：mock 计数器为 0（测试 `statistics_dry_run_zero_operation_write`）。
- `--dry-run` zero journal writes：mock 计数器为 0（测试 `statistics_dry_run_zero_journal_write`）。
- `--dry-run` zero snapshot writes：mock 计数器为 0（测试 `statistics_dry_run_zero_snapshot_write`）。
- 成功 `--json` `export_id` 字段（测试 `statistics_success_json_export_id`）。
- 成功 `--json` `artifact_set_id` 字段（测试 `statistics_success_json_artifact_set_id`）。
- 成功 `--json` `namespace` 字段（测试 `statistics_success_json_namespace`）。
- 成功 `--json` `object_type` 字段存在（测试 `statistics_success_json_object_type_field`）。
- 成功 `--json` `object_type=run_usage`（测试 `statistics_success_json_object_type_value`）。
- 成功 `--json` `oid` 字段（测试 `statistics_success_json_oid`）。
- 成功 `--json` `size` 字段（测试 `statistics_success_json_size`）。
- 成功 `--json` `dry_run=false` 字段（测试 `statistics_success_json_dry_run_false`）。
- dry-run `--json` 增 `envelope`：dry-run 载荷含信封 JSON（测试 `statistics_dry_run_json_includes_envelope`）。
- dry-run preview `artifact_set_id=export_id`（测试 `statistics_dry_run_json_artifact_set_id_matches_export_id`）。
- dry-run preview `oid` 是新 UUID（测试 `statistics_dry_run_json_oid_is_new_uuid`）。
- dry-run preview `oid` 未上传（测试 `statistics_dry_run_json_oid_is_unuploaded`）。
- dry-run preview `size=envelope.as_bytes().len()`（测试 `statistics_dry_run_json_size_matches_envelope`）。
- `--machine` ndjson：断言走全局 ndjson 规则（测试 `statistics_machine_output_ndjson`）。
- 人读输出（上传）：非 dry-run 为单行 `pushed artifact_set_id=… oid=…`（测试 `statistics_human_readable_upload`）。
- 人读 dry-run 摘要格式：输出 `dry-run export_id=… sessions=N bytes=B`（测试 `statistics_human_readable_dry_run_summary_format`）。
- 人读 dry-run `export_id` 来自 `PushOutcome.export_id`（测试 `statistics_human_readable_dry_run_export_id_source`）。
- 人读 dry-run `sessions=N` 来自 `envelope.sessions.len()`（测试 `statistics_human_readable_dry_run_sessions_source`）。
- 人读 dry-run `bytes=B` 来自 envelope UTF-8 字节数（测试 `statistics_human_readable_dry_run_bytes_source`）。
- 人读 dry-run 不含 `pushed` 字样（测试 `statistics_human_readable_dry_run_not_pushed`）。

**D 组远端后置门:** 本卡为 independent patch 发布，D-1/D-2a/D-2b/D-3 在 GO/NO-GO 两分支均强制；`complete` 前必须全绿。

**Dependencies:** `CS-14`（本地 closeout audit package）。

**Deliverables:** release note、patch commit/tag、版本面与 Cargo.lock、远端 D 组证据、本文和 `plan-status.md` 的最终收口记录。

**Implementation write set:** `docs/development/plan/issues/500.md`、`docs/development/plan/plan-status.md`

**Release write set:** 版本面三处 + `Cargo.lock` + `CHANGELOG.md` + 本计划 + `plan-status.md` + `tests/compat-ledger/SURFACES.gen`（仅恢复可执行模式 `100755`，ER-13 gate 修复；无源码内容改动）。

**Files likely touched:** Implementation write set + Release write set（本卡执行记录中的 test-only mode repair 亦须计入）。

**Docs and compatibility impact:** 无用户命令或行为变化；CHANGELOG/GitHub Release note 仅说明计划/状态与版本收口；COMPATIBILITY DOES/DOES-NOT 不变。测试生成器模式恢复是内部验证基线修复。

**Rollback mode:** `immutable-release`（已发布 tag/artifact 不可撤回；失败后一律前滚新 patch，不回退已推送 tag/artifact）。

**Migration and rollback:** N/A。

**Security and privacy:** N/A（不粘贴 token 或 transcript）。

**Performance budget:** N/A（只跑构建与测试）。

**Estimated scope:** `S`

**Version increment:** `patch`

**Release boundary:** `independent`

**C/D coverage from:** `self`（唯一收口发布卡）。

**Granularity:** `type=release; axis=closeout immutable patch release and D-group remote gates; recovery=immutable-release; complete=yes; self-contained=yes; AC=11/12; VER=11/12; landing=0; prod-files=0; scope=S; deps=CS-14; writeset=序列化于 CS-14; release=independent; split-from=CS-14; exception=N/A`

## 测试矩阵

| 类别 | 必须覆盖 | Target / command |
|---|---|---|
| 单元 | artifacts 客户端 discovery、`/sets` listing、manifest 与 resolve-file、presigned PUT header、signed GET `mode=link`/URL validation/精确 size、oid 不复用、commit 双未知失败回显清单 | `cargo nextest run --lib -E 'test(/^internal::protocol::mega2_artifacts::/)'` |
| 部署资格 | CS-16/19 对 metadata/object 完整 read surface 的 anonymous、unauthorized-cross-repo、unauthorized-cross-team DENY；CS-17 revoke/幂等/shared oid | Mega2 upstream-owned pre-GO qualification harness；不依赖 Libra implementation |
| 集成 | `statistics push` dry-run / token / 409 / 输出契约 | `cargo nextest run --test command_test -E 'test(/^command::statistics_test::/)'` |
| 集成 | 信封字段与路径/邮箱脱敏、LIMIT 10001 | `cargo nextest run --lib -E 'test(/^internal::stats_export::/)'` |
| 兼容 | help EXAMPLES、命令文档、矩阵对齐 | `compat_help_examples_banner`；`compat_command_docs_examples_section`；`compat_matrix_alignment` |
| 迁移 | N/A：无 schema writer | N/A |
| 安全 | 无 token 零 HTTP；无 usage INSERT | GC-CS-01 守卫；statistics_test |
| 性能 | LIMIT 10001 + 1 MiB | `--lib internal::stats_export` |
| live/gated | 真实 Mega2 artifacts 写 | `DEFER-CS-09`:`source .env.test && source .env.live-test && cargo nextest run --features test-network --test network_remotes_test -E 'test(mega2_artifacts_live_push)'`(env 门控；L1 mock 仍强制；live 用例须成功后依 DEP-500-13 撤销测试 set，并验证 list/get 不可读、二次撤销成功及共享 oid 保持可读) |

## 性能与容量摘要

| 路径 | 规模假设 | 上限 | 超限行为 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 包装 | 每仓 session 行 | 10_000 行且 ≤1 MiB | 失败；不提供 `--since`（DEFER） |
| 上传 | 1 blob / 命令 | 4 HTTP 回合（commit 结果未知/5xx 时至多 +1 幂等 recommit） | 不 blind retry（不重新 PUT、不换 id） |

查询负载不在范围（DEFER-CS-01）。artifacts list/get 已在 pin 存在，不构成本计划缺口。

## 故障恢复矩阵

| 故障点 | 可接受残留 | 恢复动作 | 禁止结果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| committed set 含错误/敏感数据，或 live 用例在 commit 后失败 | 单一远端 set 可能仍可 list/get | 停用后续 writer；操作者按 ADR-CS-12 / DEP-500-13 使用完整 `(repo, namespace, object_type, artifact_set_id)` 执行幂等 revoke；确认 list/get 不可读；共享 oid 由引用保护，GC 只回收超过 grace 的无引用 blob | 仅降级客户端但保留错误 set；删除仍被其它 set 引用的 oid；无补偿能力仍发布 |
| batch 已 PUT、commit **结果未知** | 可能已 commit 或 orphan | **同一** `(artifact_set_id, files)` 再 commit 一次；仍未知 → `LBR-NET-002` 失败并回显完整清单（服务端凭清单核对）；若确认已错误 commit，再走本表首行补偿 | 把超时当新数据集；静默成功 |
| batch 已 PUT、commit **明确失败** | 远端未引用 blob（orphan） | 换新 oid/`export_id` 重试；不删除远端（DEFER-CS-04） | 把部分上传报告为成功 |
| 409 清单冲突 | 无新 set | 失败；新 `export_id` | 覆盖已提交 set |
| 无 token | 零 HTTP | 提示 token | 匿名写 |
| 包装超限 | 无网络 | 本地错误 | `ok:true` 截断 |
| 5xx | 可能 orphan blob | commit 阶段 5xx/超时按「结果未知」处理：同一 `(artifact_set_id, files)` 幂等 recommit 一次；batch/PUT 阶段 5xx 即失败 | 静默成功；换 id 重 PUT |
| D 组 release 红 | 已发布失败 artifact | 前滚 FIX-* | 撤回 tag |

## 实施证据汇总

（2026-10-05 执行阶段填写。）

> **执行结论：NO-GO。** 本地 trunk compose deployment 真实探测确认 `run_usage` 闭集与 artifacts 写路径可用；read-authorization 硬门失败：anonymous principals 对 set listing/manifest/path-resolution/object GET/HEAD 可读取（200）；测试环境未配置 distinct cross-repo/team principals，这些 identity-specific probes 明确记 `not-run`；pin `cead1d8` 没有 committed-set revoke route（DELETE 405）。CS-16/19/17 均 `not-qualified` → CS-20 aggregate `not-qualified` → CS-00 `NO-GO`。维护者尚未确认 `agent_session` 或 aggregation ownership，CS-10/11/12/13 正式延入 DEFER-CS-02/03/07/10；CS-18 登记 outgoing handoff；CS-14/15 收口。

### CS-16 evidence（deployed metadata-read authorization qualification）

**逐 probe ledger（本地 manual HTTP run；部署 `http://127.0.0.1:9000`，Mega2 `cead1d8` / Cargo `0.40.14`）：**

| Probe | Outcome | Evidence / blocker |
|---|---|---|
| Harness availability / revision / run ID | `unavailable` / `not-qualified` | DEP-500-14 requires an upstream-owned versioned harness, not delivered. This local curl session provides manual observations only; harness revision/upstream run ID unavailable, local evidence label `local-manual`. |
| Harness independence | `pass` | 请求由 curl 直接调用部署 API；未调用 CS-11/12/13。 |
| Target URL / service revision | `pass` | `http://127.0.0.1:9000` / `cead1d8d7424e4756eb9fa41c7de88355c28d5d8`, Cargo `0.40.14`。 |
| Authorized P07 `GET /sets` | `pass` | Bearer principal label `agent-ci` → 200；set1 与 set2-shared 可列出。 |
| Authorized P08 `GET /sets/{set1}` | `pass` | `agent-ci` → 200；manifest 返回 path/oid/size。 |
| Authorized P09 `GET /resolve-file` | `pass` | `agent-ci` → 200；返回 artifact_set_id/path/oid/size。 |
| Anonymous `GET /sets` | `fail` | 200；披露两个 set ID 与 metadata。 |
| Anonymous `GET /sets/{set1}` | `fail` | 200；披露 manifest。 |
| Anonymous `GET /resolve-file` | `fail` | 200；披露 file metadata。 |
| Unauthorized-repo `GET /sets` | `not-run` | Manual no-credential request returned 200 empty; no repo-scoped unauthorized principal is provisioned, so this is not a valid cross-repo identity probe. |
| Unauthorized-repo `GET /sets/{set1}` | `not-run` | Manual no-credential request returned 404; no repo-scoped unauthorized principal is provisioned, so this is anonymous resource absence, not an identity probe. |
| Unauthorized-repo `GET /resolve-file` | `not-run` | Manual no-credential request returned 404; no repo-scoped unauthorized principal is provisioned, so this is anonymous resource absence, not an identity probe. |
| Unauthorized-team `GET /sets` | `not-run` | Compose provides only the `agent-ci` whole-repository token; no second team-scoped principal is provisioned. Invalid-token request returns 200 and cannot stand in for a team identity. |
| Unauthorized-team `GET /sets/{set1}` | `not-run` | Same blocker: no distinct unauthorized team principal. |
| Unauthorized-team `GET /resolve-file` | `not-run` | Same blocker: no distinct unauthorized team principal. |
| Aggregate | `not-qualified` | Anonymous read probes fail; cross-repo and cross-team identity probes are not-run because scoped principals are not configured; upstream harness is unavailable. |

### CS-19 evidence（deployed object GET/HEAD authorization qualification）

**逐 probe ledger（同一 deployment/version；manual curl，不调用 Libra 实现卡）：**

| Probe | Outcome | Evidence / blocker |
|---|---|---|
| Harness availability / revision / run ID | `unavailable` / `not-qualified` | DEP-500-14 upstream-owned harness not delivered; manual HTTP observations only; harness revision/upstream run ID unavailable, local label `local-manual`. |
| Harness independence | `pass` | 直接调用 HTTP API 与 signed storage URL。 |
| Target URL / service revision | `pass` | `http://127.0.0.1:9000` / `cead1d8d7424e4756eb9fa41c7de88355c28d5d8`, Cargo `0.40.14`。 |
| Authorized object GET link + signed fixture download | `pass` | `agent-ci` GET mode=link 200；同一 signed URL 下载 fixture 200，155 bytes。 |
| Authorized object HEAD | `pass` | `agent-ci` → 200，content-length/etag/content-type。 |
| Anonymous object GET | `fail` | mode=link → 200，返回 download action。 |
| Anonymous object HEAD | `fail` | → 200，返回对象 metadata headers。 |
| Unauthorized-repo object GET | `not-run` | Manual no-credential request returned 404; no repo-scoped unauthorized principal configured, so it is not a valid cross-repo identity probe. |
| Unauthorized-repo object HEAD | `not-run` | Manual no-credential request returned 404; no repo-scoped unauthorized principal configured. |
| Unauthorized-team object GET | `not-run` | No distinct unauthorized team-scoped principal configured; invalid token is not a valid team identity probe. |
| Unauthorized-team object HEAD | `not-run` | Same blocker: no distinct unauthorized team-scoped principal configured. |
| Aggregate | `not-qualified` | Anonymous GET/HEAD disclose data; cross-repo and cross-team identity probes are not-run because those principals are not configured; upstream harness unavailable. |

### CS-17 evidence（deployed set revoke / shared object qualification）

**P01..P24 per-probe ledger（deployment `http://127.0.0.1:9000`, `cead1d8`, local manual HTTP run；不得把 `not-run` 当 pass）：**

| Probe | Outcome | Evidence / blocker |
|---|---|---|
| P01 harness-availability | `unavailable` / `not-qualified` | Required upstream-owned/versioned harness not delivered (DEP-500-14); local manual curl is an observation only. |
| P02 harness-independence | `pass` | Direct curl to Mega2; no Libra implementation/test binary. |
| P03 deployment-url | `pass` | `http://127.0.0.1:9000`. |
| P04 service-version | `pass` | `cead1d8d7424e4756eb9fa41c7de88355c28d5d8`, Cargo `0.40.14`. |
| P05 harness-revision | `unavailable` / `not-run` | No upstream-owned harness revision delivered. |
| P06 run-id | `unavailable` / `not-run` | No upstream qualification run ID; local observation label `local-manual`. |
| P07 seed repo | `pass` | `probeorg/project`. |
| P08 seed namespace | `pass` | `libra/stats-export/v1`. |
| P09 seed object_type | `pass` | `run_usage`. |
| P10 seed artifact_set_id | `pass` | `set1-1791197541`; companion `set2-shared-1791198099`. |
| P11 seed setup | `pass` | `agent-ci` GET `/sets` and GET `/sets/set1-1791197541` returned 200; fixture manifest visible. |
| P12 revoke tuple/entrypoint | `fail` | Exact target `(probeorg/project, libra/stats-export/v1, run_usage, set1-1791197541)` targeted; DELETE route returned 405, so no successful revoke. |
| P13 revoke/list after revoke | `not-run` | Blocked by P12; no revoke occurred. Pre-revoke liveness: second set remains listed. |
| P14 revoked manifest | `not-run` | Blocked by P12; pre-revoke same-principal manifest positive read 200. Baseline manifest SHA-256 `30d069cfdd0b972e66f6c04d2cbafc1d4d0e6f4a1e7014868f7d35503c0745fa`; post-revoke comparison cannot run. |
| P15 revoked resolve | `not-run` | Blocked by P12; pre-revoke resolve positive read 200, same path resolves oid `5098d18d-1b93-4de1-90d1-1b8a5976c8c5`. |
| P16 unshared GET | `not-run` | Blocked by P12; current fixture oid is shared, and no unshared seed can be generated without write/revoke fixture procedure. |
| P17 unshared HEAD | `not-run` | Same blocker as P16. |
| P18 signed URL | `not-qualified` | Pre-revoke positive control: `GET mode=link` 200; capability URL was not persisted (security: signed URL is a bearer capability); redacted location `http://rustfs:9000/mega2/artifact/50/98/d1/8d-1b93-4de1-90d1-1b8a5976c8c5?<redacted>`, SHA-256 `96a27b0888fe7eb5c46af9214570266495b2faf5f0ec2d5d53f07d9a91783f5e`; same URL fetched fixture (200, 155 bytes), expiry `2026-10-05T20:28:43.053Z`. P12 revoke unavailable, so post-revoke branch `not-run`. |
| P19 anonymous revoke denial | `fail` | Ordered before the final P12 attempt. At `2026-10-05T19:31:08Z`, anonymous DELETE returned 405 (route absent, not 401/403). P11 principal `agent-ci` intact check immediately before/after: list digest `48789b9a16b88785c99e1cd9bffdf0bd4133ff4bab5f4cc1f18cf25c0911e679`, manifest digest `30d069cfdd0b972e66f6c04d2cbafc1d4d0e6f4a1e7014868f7d35503c0745fa`, equal before/after. The authorized P12 DELETE attempt followed in the same second and returned 405; no revoke occurred. |
| P20 unauthorized-repo revoke denial | `not-run` | No repo-scoped unauthorized principal is configured; the no-credential other-repo DELETE returned 405 due absent route and does not represent an authenticated unauthorized-repo principal probe. |
| P21 unauthorized-team revoke denial | `not-run` | No second team-scoped principal configured; invalid token is not a valid team identity, and route is absent. |
| P22 repeated revoke | `not-run` | No initial revoke success (P12). |
| P23 shared oid after revoke | `not-run` | Pre-revoke P11 principal GET mode=link and signed download returned 200/155 fixture bytes; set was never revoked, so post-revoke shared-object preservation cannot run. |
| P24 aggregate | `not-qualified` | Non-pass: P01/P12/P18 post-revoke/P19; not-run: P05/P06/P13-P18 post-revoke/P20-P23. |

- **P01 harness availability / revision / run ID:** upstream-owned harness unavailable (DEP-500-14 not delivered); local manual curl only; revision/upstream run ID unavailable.
- **Local observation target:** `http://127.0.0.1:9000`, Mega2 `cead1d8` / Cargo `0.40.14`.

- **CS-19 outcome：** `not-qualified`；URL/version 一致。
- **CS-17 outcome：** `not-qualified`；URL/version 一致。

### CS-00 evidence（decision）

- **判定：** `NO-GO`。
- **理由：** ① CS-20 aggregate=`not-qualified`（storage-only read surface publicly exposes list/manifest/path and object data: anonymous set/manifest/resolve/object GET/HEAD return 200; committed-set revoke route is absent (`DELETE` 405)); ② the available compose config has no distinct cross-repo/team-scoped principals, so those ACL identity probes are `not-run`; ③ maintainer 2026-09-21 comment identifies Mega2 only, not `agent_session`, and the later two-question inquiry remains unanswered. These are sufficient blockers for NO-GO; no unsupported cross-team result is claimed.
- **免于 false-qualified 的关键观察：** 本地部署同时演示了 `run_usage` 闭集（discovery `supported_object_types` 含 `run_usage`）与写路径可用（batch→PUT(fallback)→commit 成功，单一 blob path `stats/export/v1/stats.export.v1.json`，oid 为 UUID，两 `artifact_set_id` 并存，跨 set 共享 oid）。这样即使将来引入正确读 ACL/revoke，行为机契约仍成立；但其时需另行 qualification。
